title: 6 bands tartan setts (page 4)
date: 2023-03-18
weight: 6
---
1,426 setts across 1,330 tartans with 6 bands, grouped by colour-sequence pattern. Patterns with fewer than 40 setts are listed in full below; busier ones link to their own page. Each variant shows ΔT — its mean distance (OKLab ΔE) from the pattern's base colours — nearest first, with a stripe bar in the nearest human-palette colour names (hover a stripe to read it).
